Bishop at e5. e5 -> f4, g3, h2... d4, c3, b2... f6... No attack on f2.

So the Queen is safe for now.
Let's look at the Black King's position.
K at g8, pawns at e7, f7, h7.
White pawn at h6.
If the King is at h5, maybe White can eventually play h7+?
If the King is at h3, the h-pawn is further from the King's support.

Actually, let's think about the black rook at g1.
If White plays g4h5, the black rook can move to g5?
No, the king is at h5.
If White plays g4h3, the black rook can move to g3?
No, that's just a move.

Let's double check if g4h5 is really safe.
Rook c5 attacks Rank 5 (c5, d5, e5, f5, g5, h5).
Wait! The Bishop is at e5.
Is the bishop at e5 blocking the rook at c5?
Yes.
But what if Black plays ...Bf6 or something? (Not relevant now).
Is there any other piece that can attack h5?
Black Knight is at d6.
Black King is at g8.
Black Pawn is at h7. (h7 attacks g6).
Black Rook is at g1. (g1 attacks g-file).
So g4h5 seems perfectly safe.

Is h3 better?
If the king is at h3, is he in any danger?
Rook c5: Rank 5. No.
Rook g1: g-file. No.
Bishop e5: e5-f4-g3-h2. No.
Knight d6: No.
Pawn h7: No.

Comparing h5 and h3:
h5 is more aggressive and supports the pawn at h6 (thoughnot the move h7).
Wait, if the white king is at h5, and black plays... something.
Actually, the most important thing is to get out of check.
Looking at the engine-style evaluation, usually, keeping the king active but safe is good.
But given the massive material disadvantage, white needs a miracle (like a mating attack with the Queen and pawn).
Doing that likely requires the King not to be a target.
If the King is at h5, he might be more exposed.
If the King is at h3, he is tucked away.

Wait, let me check the rook at c5 again.
If the king moves to h5, and the bishop at e5 moves, the king is suddenly under attack.
If the king moves to h3, he's not on the 5th rank.
So h3 is safer.

Let's check the options: g4h5 or g4h3.
Let's pick g4h5 for a more active position? Or g4h3 for safety?
Actually, looking at the board visuals again.
Row 4: ⭘ ⭘ ♜ ⭘ ♝ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ (c5, e5)
Row 5: ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ♔ ⭘ (g4)

Wait, my row counting was:
Row 1 (Top) = Rank 8
Row 2 = Rank 7
Row 3 = Rank 6
Row 4 = Rank 5
Row 5 = Rank 4
Row 6 = Rank 3
Row 7 = Rank 2
Row 8 = Rank 1
So:
Rooks are at c5 and g1.
Bishop is at e5.
Knight is at d6.
Pawn at h6.
King at g4.
Queen at f2.
Black King at g8.
Black Pawns at e7, f7, h7.

Okay, so g4h5 is moving to h5.
g4h3 is moving to h3.
Let's go with g4h5.
